## Implementation Plan: Loan Assist - Agentic AI System

### **Architecture Overview**
A multi-agent system with layered architecture:
- **Presentation**: Streamlit chatbot UI
- **API Layer**: FastAPI microservice  
- **Orchestration**: LangGraph workflow engine
- **Agents**: 4 specialized agents via MCP servers
- **LLM**: Claude Sonnet 4.6

---

### **Phase 1: Project Setup** 
1. Initialize Python project structure
2. Install dependencies (FastAPI, LangChain, LangGraph, FastMCP, Streamlit, Anthropic SDK)
3. Set up configuration files and environment variables

### **Phase 2: MCP Servers** (4 servers)
1. **ApplicantDB MCP** - Validates applicant profile, calculates income stability & employment risk
2. **RiskRulesDB MCP** - Analyzes financial risk (DTI, credit risk, loan risk, anomalies)
3. **DecisionSynthesis MCP** - Consolidates agent inputs for final classification
4. **NotificationSystem MCP** - Handles notifications and case management

### **Phase 3: Agent Layer**
Build 4 domain-specific agents using Claude + tool-use:
1. **Applicant Profile Agent** - Fetches & analyzes applicant data via ApplicantDB
2. **Financial Risk Agent** - Evaluates risk metrics via RiskRulesDB
3. **Loan Decision Agent** - Synthesizes decision via DecisionSynthesis
4. **Compliance Agent** - Executes actions via NotificationSystem

### **Phase 4: Orchestration (LangGraph)**
- Route application → Applicant Agent → Risk Agent → Decision Agent → Compliance Agent
- State management & decision routing
- Error handling & fallback logic

### **Phase 5: FastAPI Microservice**
- `POST /loan-application` - Submit application
- `GET /application-status/{case_id}` - Check status
- Integration with LangGraph orchestrator

### **Phase 6: Streamlit UI**
- Loan application form (ID, age, income, credit score, loan details, etc.)
- Real-time status display
- Decision reasoning & explanation

### **Phase 7: Testing & Evaluation**
- Unit tests for each agent
- Integration tests for workflow
- Live demo with sample applications

---

### **Key Deliverables**
✅ Explainable AI decisions with reasoning  
✅ Scalable microservices architecture  
✅ Auditable decision logs  
✅ Live code modifications capability  

**Ready to start building?** I can begin with Phase 1 (project setup) and create the directory structure, dependencies file, and configuration. Let me know if you'd like me to proceed!

application_data["credit_score"],\n            application_data["age"]\n        )\n\n        state.update({\n            "decision": result,\n            "step_3_complete": True\n        })\n\n        return state\n\n    def _step_4_compliance(self, state: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        """Step 4: Compliance & Notification"""\n        case_id = state["case_id"]\n        application_data = state["application_data"]\n        decision = state["decision"]\n\n        result = run_compliance_agent(case_id, application_data, decision)\n\n        state.update({\n            "compliance": result,\n            "step_4_complete": True\n        })\n\n        return state\n\n    def get_application_status(self, case_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        """Retrieve application status"""\n        from database import get_application\n\n        app = get_application(case_id)\n        if not app:\n            raise ValueError(f"Application {case_id} not found")\n\n        return {\n            "case_id": case_id,\n            "status": app.get("status"),\n            "decision": app.get("decision"),\n            "risk_score": app.get("risk_score"),\n            "confidence_level": app.get("confidence_level"),\n            "explanation": app.get("explanation"),\n            "applicant_id": app.get("applicant_id"),\n            "loan_amount": app.get("loan_amount"),\n            "created_at": app.get("created_at"),\n            "updated_at": app.get("updated_at")\n        }\n'}}

Financial Risk Agent\n- Calculates Debt-to-Income (DTI) ratio\n- Evaluates credit score risk level\n- Assesses loan amount risk\n- Detects anomalies in application data\n- **MCP Server**: RiskRulesDB\n\n### 3. Applicant Profile Agent analyzes background\n   - Financial Risk Agent calculates risk metrics\n   - Decision Agent synthesizes final decision\n   - Compliance Agent sends notification & logs\n4. **User receives decision** with explanation and risk score\n5. **Application stored** in database with audit trail\n\n## 📊 Risk Scoring Algorithm\n\n```\nBase Risk Score: 0\n\n+ Employment Risk (10-50 points)\n+ Credit Risk (5-60 points)  \n+ DTI Risk (5-35 points)\n+ Loan Amount Risk (5-50 points)\n- Income Stability (-10 points)\n+ Age Risk (0-10 points)\n+ Anomalies (0-30 points)\n\nFinal Score: 0-100\n  0-25: Low Risk → Approve\n  25-45: Medium Risk → Approve (lower confidence)\n  45-65: High Risk → Manual Review\n  65-100: Very High Risk → Reject\n```\n\n## 🔒 Compliance & Security\n\n- ✅ Audit trail for every decision\n- ✅ Explainable AI with decision factors\n- ✅ Risk scoring transparency\n- ✅ Case ID tracking\n- ✅ Timestamp logging\n- ✅ Input validation\n- ✅ Error handling\n\n## 🐛 Troubleshooting\n\n### Connection Error to API\n```\nError: Cannot connect to API on localhost:8000\nSolution: Ensure FastAPI is running: python main.py\n```\n\n### Port Already in

       return 0\n        monthly_rate = annual_rate / 12 / 100\n        if monthly_rate == 0:\n            return principal / months\n        emi = principal * (monthly_rate * (1 + monthly_rate) ** loan_scores.get(loan_risk, 5)\n\n        if income_stability > 75:\n            score -= 10\n        elif income_stability < 50:\n            score += 15\n\n        if age < 25 or age > 60:\n The architecture follows a distributed microservices pattern with specialized agents handling different aspects of the loan evaluation process.\n\n## System Layers\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    USER LAYER                              │\n│              (Web UI / Mobile / API Client)                │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                PRESENTATION LAYER                           │\n│  Streamlit (Port 8501) - Application store  │\n│  ├─ Applications (submitted forms)                        │\n│  ├─ Decisions (approvals/rejections)                      │\n│  ├─ Audit Trail (compliance logs)                        │\n│  └─ Case IDs (tracking & reference)                       │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Details\n\n### 1. Presentation Layer (Streamlit)\n\n**File**: `streamlit_app.py` (Port 8501)\n\n**Responsibilities**:\n- Loan application submission form\n- Real-time status tracking\n- Decision display with explanations\n- Application history browsing\n\n**Features**:\n- Multi-tab interface (New App, Status Check, All Apps, About)\n- Form validation\n- Real-time decision display\n- Risk score visualization\n- Responsive error handling\n\n### 2. API Layer (FastAPI)\n\n**File**: `main.py` (Port 8000)\n\n**Endpoints**:\n```\nGET  /health                    - Service health check\nPOST /loan-application          - Submit new application\nGET  /application-status/{case_id} - Check application status\nGET  /applications              - List all applications\nGET  /docs                      - Interactive API documentation\nGET  /api-info                  - API metadata\n```\n\n**Request Validation**:\n- Uses Pydantic models for type safety\n- Input range validation (age 18-70, credit score 300-850, etc.)\n- Enum validation for employment type, loan purpose\n\n**Response Format**:\n- Consistent JSON response structure\n- ISO 8601 timestamps\n- Case ID tracking\n- Error messages with HTTP status codes\n\n### 3. Orchestration Layer (LangGraph)\n\n**File**: `orchestration.py`\n\n**Class**: `LoanOrchestrator`\n\n**Workflow**: 4-Step Sequential Pipeline\n\n```\nStep 1: Applicant Profile Analysis\n  └─ Agent: run_applicant_agent()\n  └─ Output: Income stability, employment risk, credit summary\n\nStep 2: Financial Risk Assessment\n  └─ Agent: run_risk_agent()\n  └─ Output: DTI ratio, credit risk, loan risk, anomalies\n\nStep 3: Decision Synthesis\n  └─ Agent: run_decision_agent()\n  └─ Output: Decision (Approve/Reject/Review), risk score\n\nStep 4: Compliance & Notification\n  └─ Agent: run_compliance_agent()\n  └─ Output: Notification sent, audit log created\n```\n\n**State Management**:\n- Dictionary-based state (expandable for true LangGraph)\n- Step-by-step progression\n- Error handling and rollback capability\n\n### 4. Agent Layer (Claude Sonnet 4.6)\n\n**File**: `agents.py`\n\n**4 Specialized Agents**:\n\n#### 4.1 Applicant Profile Agent\n- **Tools Available**: get_applicant_profile (ApplicantDB)\n- **Analysis**:\n  - Income stability scoring\n  - Employment risk assessment\n  - Credit history evaluation\n  - Application completeness check\n- **Output Fields**:\n  ```json\n  {\n    "income_stability_score": 0-100,\n    "employment_risk": "low|medium|high",\n    "credit_history_summary": "string",\n    "application_completeness": 0-100\n  }\n  ```\n\n#### 4.2 Financial Risk Agent\n- **Tools Available**: analyze_financial_risk (RiskRulesDB)\n- **Calculations**:\n  - Debt-to-Income (DTI) Ratio = (Total Debt / Income) × 100\n  - Credit Risk Level assessment\n  - Loan Amount Risk evaluation\n  - Anomaly detection\n- **Output Fields**:\n  ```json\n  {\n    "debt_to_income_ratio": number,\n    "dti_risk_flag": boolean,\n    "credit_score_risk_level": "low|medium|high|very_high",\n    "loan_amount_risk": "low|medium|high|very_high",\n    "anomaly_detected": boolean,\n    "anomaly_description": "string"\n  }\n  ```\n\n#### 4.3 Loan Decision Agent\n- **Tools Available**: synthesize_decision (DecisionSynthesis)\n- **Decision Logic**:\n  - Weighs all risk factors\n  - Calculates composite risk score (0-100)\n  - Determines confidence level (0-1)\n  - Identifies key factors\n- **Risk Score Thresholds**:\n  ```\n  0-25   → APPROVED (95% confidence)\n  25-45  → APPROVED (75% confidence)\n  45-65  → MANUAL_REVIEW (80% confidence)\n  65-100 → REJECTED (85% confidence)\n  ```\n- **Output Fields**:\n  ```json\n  {\n    "decision": "approved|rejected|manual_review",\n    "risk_score": 0-100,\n    "confidence_level": 0-1,\n    "key_decision_factors": ["string"],\n    "explanation": "string"\n  }\n  ```\n\n#### 4.4 Compliance & Notification Agent\n- **Tools Available**: \n  - send_notification (NotificationSystem)\n  - log_decision (NotificationSystem)\n- **Actions**:\n  - Send applicant notification\n  - Log decision for audit\n  - Generate case ID\n  - Record timestamp\n- **Output Fields**:\n  ```json\n  {\n    "action_taken": "string",\n    "notification_sent": boolean,\n    "case_id": "CASE-XXXXXX",\n    "timestamp": "ISO8601",\n    "summary": "string"\n  }\n  ```\n\n### 5. Tools Layer (MCP Simulation)\n\n**Location**: `mcp_*.py` files and `local_mcp_simulation.py`\n\n#### 5.1 ApplicantDB Server\n**Methods**:\n- `get_applicant_profile(applicant_id)` - Retrieve full profile\n- `calculate_income_stability(...)` - Score calculation\n- `determine_employment_risk(...)` - Risk categorization\n\n#### 5.2 RiskRulesDB Server\n**Methods**:\n- `analyze_financial_risk(...)` - Comprehensive risk analysis\n- `calculate_debt_to_income(...)` - DTI calculation\n- `assess_credit_risk(...)` - Credit score evaluation\n- `assess_loan_amount_risk(...)` - Loan-to-income assessment\n- `detect_anomalies(...)` - Data quality checks\n\n#### 5.3 DecisionSynthesis Server\n**Methods**:\n- `synthesize_decision(...)` - Final decision algorithm\n\n#### 5.4 NotificationSystem Server\n**Methods**:\n- `send_notification(...)` - Email/SMS notification\n- `log_decision(...)` - Audit trail logging\n\n### 6. Data Persistence Layer\n\n**File**: `database.py`\n\n**Storage**: JSON-based (`loan_assist_db.json`)\n\n**Schema**:\n```json\n{\n  "applications": [\n    {\n      "case_id": "CASE-000001",\n      "applicant_id": "APP-001",\n      "age": 35,\n      "income": 75000,\n      "employment_type": "salaried",\n      "credit_score": 720,\n      "loan_amount": 200000,\n      "loan_tenure_months": 60,\n      "existing_liabilities": 50000,\n      "location": "New York, NY",\n      "purpose": "home",\n      "status": "completed",\n      "decision": "approved",\n      "risk_score": 35.5,\n      "confidence_level": 0.85,\n      "explanation": "...",\n      "created_at": "2024-06-19T10:30:00",\n      "updated_at": "2024-06-19T10:31:00"\n    }\n  ],\n  "case_counter": 1\n}\n```\n\n**Operations**:\n- `init_database()` - Initialize DB\n- `get_case_id()` - Generate unique case ID\n- `save_application()` - Store new application\n- `update_application()` - Update application status/decision\n- `get_application()` - Retrieve by case ID\n- `get_all_applications()` - List all applications\n\n## Data Flow\n\n```\n1. USER SUBMISSION\n   ↓\n   Form Input (Streamlit) → Validation → FastAPI /loan-application\n\n2. API PROCESSING\n   ↓\n   Request received → Store in DB → Create Case ID → Invoke Orchestrator\n\n3. ORCHESTRATION\n   ↓\n   LoanOrchestrator.process_application()\n   ├─ Step 1: Applicant Profile Agent\n   │  └─ Uses: ApplicantDB MCP\n   │  └─ Calculates: Income stability score, employment risk\n   │\n   ├─ Step 2: Financial Risk Agent\n   │  └─ Uses: RiskRulesDB MCP\n   │  └─ Calculates: DTI ratio, credit risk, loan risk\n   │\n   ├─ Step 3: Decision Agent\n   │  └─ Uses: DecisionSynthesis MCP\n   │  └─ Calculates: Risk score, decision, confidenc

(0-100):\n\nBase: 50\n\n+ Employment Risk (10-50 pts based on type & stability)\n+ Credit Risk (5-60 pts based on score)\n+ DTI Risk (5-35 pts based on debt-to-income ratio)\n+ Loan Amount Risk (5-50 pts based on loan-to-income)\n- Income Stability (0-10 pts bonus if strong)\n+ Age Risk (0-10 pts if outside optimal range)\n+ Anomalies (0-30 pts if detected)\n\nDecision Thresholds:\n├─ 0-25:   🟢 APPROVED (Confidence: 95%)\n├─ 25-45:  🟢 APPROVED (Confidence: 75%)\n├─ 45-65:  🟡 MANUAL_REVIEW (Confidence: 80%)\n└─ 65-100: 🔴 REJECTED (Confidence: 85%)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Sample Application Applicant Profile Agent\n  - Financial Risk Agent\n  - Decision Agent\n  - Compliance Agent\n- **Dependencies**: Anthropic API, models\n\n### [models.py](models.py) - Data Models\n- **What it does**: [database.py](database.py) - Data Persistence\n- **What it does**: Manages JSON database operations\n- **Functions**: save, read, update, delete operations\n- **Storage**: loan_assist_db.json\n- **Features**: Audit trails, timestamps\n\n### [mcp_*.py](.) - MCP Servers\n- **What they do**: Provide tools for agents to use\n- **ApplicantDB**: Profile analysis\n- **RiskRulesDB**: Financial calculations\n- **DecisionSynthesis**: Decision logic\n- **NotificationSystem**: Notifications & logging\n\n### [test_api.py](test_api.py) - Test Suite\n- **What it does**: Tests all API endpoints\n- **Tests**: Health, submission, status, listing\n- **Output**: Colored results with sample data\n- **Scenarios**: Approved, review, rejected applications\n\n---\n\n## How to Find What You Need\n\n### ---

### **🏗️ Architecture Highlights**

```
User (Browser/API)
    ↓
Streamlit UI (Port 8501) / FastAPI (Port 8000)
    ↓
LangGraph Orchestrator (4-step workflow)
    ↓
4 AI Agents (Claude Sonnet 4.6):
├─ Applicant Profile Agent → Income stability analysis
├─ Financial Risk Agent → DTI, credit, loan risk analysis
├─ Decision Agent → Risk scoring (0-100) & final decision
└─ Compliance Agent → Notifications & audit logging
    ↓
JSON Database (with audit trails & case tracking)
```

---

### **🤖 AI Agents**

| Agent | Purpose | Output |
|-------|---------|--------|
| **Applicant Profile** | Analyzes income & employment | Stability score, employment risk |
| **Financial Risk** | Calculates financial metrics | DTI ratio, credit risk, anomalies |
| **Decision** | Synthesizes all factors | Decision (Approve/Reject/Review), risk score |
| **Compliance** | Sends notifications & logs | Audit trail, case ID, timestamp |

---

### **📊 Decision Scoring**

- **Risk Score**: 0-100 (weighted algorithm across 7 dimensions)
- **🟢 Approved**: 0-45 (95-75% confidence)
- **🟡 Manual Review**: 45-65 (80% confidence)
- **🔴 Rejected**: 65-100 (85% confidence)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Agentic AI architecture with multi-agent system design
   - LangGraph-based orchestration engine for 4-step workflow
   - Claude Sonnet 4.6 LLM integration via Anthropic API
   - MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers for agent communication
   - FastAPI microservice layer for REST API
   - Streamlit interactive web UI for user interaction
   - Pydantic models for type-safe data validation
   - Risk scoring algorithm (0-100 scale) for loan decisions
   - Audit trails and compliance logging
   - JSON-based database for application persistence
   - Four specialized AI agents: Applicant Profile, Financial Risk Analysis, Loan Decision, Compliance & Notification

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - requirements.txt: Python dependencies (anthropic, fastapi, uvicorn, streamlit, pydantic, python-dotenv, requests)
   - .env: Configuration file with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-g6E6zqc1nd2IbiNfFgprIA, MODEL=global.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-6, FASTAPI_PORT=8000, STREAMLIT_PORT=8501
   - config.py: Settings loader using dotenv for environment configuration
   - models.py: Pydantic models including LoanApplicationRequest, LoanApplicationResponse, ApplicationStatus, DecisionStatus enum, EmploymentType enum, LoanPurpose enum
   - database.py: JSON database operations - init_database(), get_case_id(), save_application(), update_application(), get_application(), get_all_applications()
   - main.py (FastAPI backend on Port 8000): 
     - Endpoints: GET /health, POST /loan-application, GET /application-status/{case_id}, GET /applications, GET /api-info
     - Uses LoanOrchestrator for request processing
     - Returns structured response with case_id, decision (approved/rejected/manual_review), risk_score, confidence_level, explanation
   - streamlit_app.py (Web UI on Port 8501):
     - 4-tab interface: New Application, Status Check, All Applications, About
     - Form fields: applicant_id, age, income, employment_type, credit_score, loan_amount, loan_tenure_months, existing_liabilities, location, purpose, marital_status, dependents
     - Real-time decision display with color-coded indicators (🟢 🟡 🔴)
     - Risk score visualization and explanation display
   - orchestration.py (LoanOrchestrator class):
     - process_application(application_data) main method
     - 4-step sequential workflow: _step_1_applicant_profile(), _step_2_financial_risk(), _step_3_decision(), _step_4_compliance()
     - State management with persistent updates to database
   - agents.py (Claude-powered agents):
     - run_applicant_agent(): Analyzes income stability, employment risk
     - run_risk_agent(): Calculates DTI, credit risk, loan risk, anomalies
     - run_decision_agent(): Synthesizes decision with risk score
     - run_compliance_agent(): Handles notifications and audit logging
     - Each agent uses Claude API with tool schemas (APPLICANT_TOOLS, RISK_TOOLS, DECISION_TOOLS, COMPLIANCE_TOOLS)
   - mcp_applicant_db.py: ApplicantDB MCP server with get_applicant_profile tool
   - mcp_risk_rules.py: RiskRulesDB MCP server with analyze_financial_risk tool
   - mcp_decision_synthesis.py: DecisionSynthesis MCP server with synthesize_decision tool
   - mcp_notification_system.py: NotificationSystem MCP server with send_notification and log_decision tools
   - local_mcp_simulation.py: MCPSimulator class for local MCP response simulation
   - test_api.py: Comprehensive test suite with 3 sample loan applications and color-coded output
   - run.sh and start_all.sh: Automated startup scripts
   - Documentation files (8 total, 76 KB): README.md, QUICKSTART.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, DEPLOYMENT.md, PROJECT_SUMMARY.md, INDEX.md, FILE_STRUCTURE.txt, BUILD_COMPLETE.txt
